- **WHEN-NOT-TO-ADD**: &gt; **故意保持近空原则**：本目录仅放 **subagent 定义**。每个 `*.md` 定义一个 Task 可启动的角色，含 v2 agent-spec frontmatter。加非 agent 文件会稀释目录用途、误导 ROUTER 的角色发现。 ([jasonhnd/life_OS](https://github.com/jasonhnd/life_OS/blob/HEAD/docs/history/i18n-v1.10/zh/agents/WHEN-NOT-TO-ADD.md) · ★5)
- **when-should-langgraph-be-chosen-over-crewai-for-an-sdlc-agent-team**: LangGraph should be chosen over CrewAI when an SDLC agent team's workflow requires conditional branching, cycles, or complex runtime-dependent execution paths that cannot be predetermined — specifically when code review agents must route between multiple remediation paths, when iterative refinement loops are required, or when the workflow itself (not just the agents) is the primary engineering challenge; CrewAI remains the default for role-defined, phase-sequential SDLC pipelines where the execution path is predictable and the hard problem is agent role specification rather than control flow. ([shakedfb/deterministic-agentic-sdlc-workflow](https://github.com/shakedfb/deterministic-agentic-sdlc-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/when-should-langgraph-be-chosen-over-crewai-for-an-sdlc-agent-team.md))
- **when-to-use**: A subagent is not free. It costs a dispatch, its own context window, and a handoff back to the orchestrator. Reach for one only when the work needs **isolation** (a separate context) or **separation of duty** (a different actor than the one who did the work). Otherwise, do the work directly in the main conversation. ([ForgePlan/marketplace](https://github.com/ForgePlan/marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/cc-best/skills/cc-best/sections/agents/when-to-use.md) · ★3)

It writes test files and reports bugs it finds; it does not modify source or fix anything. - **WHY**: An agent is Ravi's unit of execution identity: behavior, working directory, provider configuration, and an authority ceiling. Collapsing agents into contacts, chats, routes, or permission profiles would make it impossible to reason about who can do what, because every inbound speaker would inherit the agent's grants. ([filipexyz/ravi](https://github.com/filipexyz/ravi/blob/HEAD/.ravi/specs/agents/WHY.md) · ★6)
- **WHY**: Agents are the identities everything else routes through: a deleted agent takes its config and routing role with it, a reset throws away session context that cannot be rebuilt, and a permissions change rewrites what the agent (and the automations running on its behalf) is allowed to do at runtime. Those are exactly the three ops that got the write brake. The rest of the surface (`create`, `set`, ` ([filipexyz/ravi](https://github.com/filipexyz/ravi/blob/HEAD/.ravi/specs/cli/agents/WHY.md) · ★6)

- **wiki-linter**: Runs semantic lint on the wiki. Invoke when the user runs /wiki-lint --semantic. Surfaces contradictions, obsolete claims, and confidence vs sources mismatches. ([agustincbajo/Coral](https://github.com/agustincbajo/Coral/blob/HEAD/template/agents/wiki-linter.md))
- **wiki-linter**: Audit the wiki for structural problems — frontmatter completeness, broken intra-wiki links, orphan pages, missing required sections — and report findings. Read-only: reports, does not fix. Use before a commit or publish. &lt;example&gt; Context: About to publish the wiki. user: "Check the wiki for structural issues before I push." assistant: "I'll dispatch wiki-linter to audit and report." &lt;commentary&gt;Read-only structural audit is this agent.&lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; ([jim-bo/knowledgizer](https://github.com/jim-bo/knowledgizer/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-linter.md))
- **wiki-linter**: Health-checks the wiki and fixes what can be auto-fixed ([liamellison02/brain-template-starter](https://github.com/liamellison02/brain-template-starter/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/wiki-linter.md))
- **wiki-linter**: Health-checks the LLM wiki. Runs link-audit scripts (orphans, broken links, backlinks) then reasons about contradictions, stale claims superseded by newer sources, concepts mentioned but lacking a page, and missing cross-references. Produces a prioritized fix list and suggests new questions/sources. Use for "wiki check / lint / health check / cleanup". ([yonghoonlee/llmwiki-harness-en](https://github.com/yonghoonlee/llmwiki-harness-en/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/wiki-linter.md))
- **wiki-lookup**: 작업 전에 호출하는 지식 vault wiki 조회 도구. 주제·영역·키워드를 받아 관련 wiki 페이지를 찾아 요약하고 백링크로 이어진 관련 페이지를 함께 반환한다. read-only — wiki 수정·코드 작성·다른 에이전트 호출은 하지 않는다. 가볍고 빈번하게 호출된다. ([ryu-qqq/terraform-modules](https://github.com/ryu-qqq/terraform-modules/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/wiki-lookup.md) · 2 copies)
- **wiki-maintainer**: Reads your spec source and writes wiki/ pages. Supervised — every edit is a PR and requires human review. ([anoop2811/software-factory-template](https://github.com/anoop2811/software-factory-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/wiki-maintainer.md) · ★8 · 2 copies)

- **wiki-maintainer**: Drains docs/wiki/.pending/changes.jsonl into wiki page updates, reconciling raw-source changes against existing wiki pages per the schema contract. &lt;example&gt; Context: The hook threshold fires after 10 pending changes accumulate user: "wiki maintenance" assistant: "Spawning wiki-maintainer to drain 10 pending changes" &lt;commentary&gt; The agent reads changes.jsonl, groups entries by path, finds owning wiki pages via raw_sources reverse-index, and updates each page. &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt; Context: A developer wants to manually sync the wiki after a large refactor user: "reconcile wiki"… ([esxr/product-wiki](https://github.com/esxr/product-wiki/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-maintainer.md) · ★1)
- **wiki-maintainer**: Maintains the aissert knowledge/ wiki after significant repository changes or wiki lint failures. ([YauheniPo/aissert](https://github.com/YauheniPo/aissert/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/wiki-maintainer.md) · ★1)
- **wiki-maintainer**: Handles complex wiki operations — batch ingest, full lint, cross-page restructuring, and deep queries requiring synthesis across many pages ([platootalp/claude-harness](https://github.com/platootalp/claude-harness/blob/HEAD/plugins/wiki/agents/wiki-maintainer.md))
- **wiki-maintainer**: Keeps a project's docs/wiki knowledge sets true without making new claims: repoints entries whose section was renamed or moved, re-reads drifted sections and rewrites or confirms their one-line summaries, and rewrites the catalogue line of sets that are offered but never opened. Every write goes through wiki_edit_set, which judges it and marks the set for a person to review afterwards. Delegate when `graphin wiki check` or `graphin wiki queue` shows dangling, drifted or unread sets. It never writes a set from nothing and never edits a document. ([Salvia95/graphin](https://github.com/Salvia95/graphin/blob/HEAD/plugin/graphin-guide/agents/wiki-maintainer.md))
- **wiki-maintainer-agent**: Update wiki pages, cross-references, index, and log after execution events, reviews, fixes, convergence results, gate decisions, and notes — keeping compiled knowledge current. - **wiki-maintainer-triggers**: Wiki Maintainer Triggers ([launchstack-dev/loom-ai](https://github.com/launchstack-dev/loom-ai/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-maintainer-triggers.md) · ★1)
- **wiki-manager**: Two modes. Gather (default): curated project knowledge for Technical Architect on HIGH-complexity tasks, spawned as a nested subagent (leaf — no Agent tool), writes wiki-brief.md. - **wiki-manager**: Two modes. Gather (default): curated project knowledge for Technical Architect on HIGH-complexity tasks, spawned as a nested subagent (leaf — no Task tool), writes wiki-brief.md. ([Community-Access/accessibility-agents](https://github.com/Community-Access/accessibility-agents/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/wiki-manager.md) · 2 copies)
- **wiki-manager**: GitHub Wiki command center -- create, edit, organize, and search wiki pages entirely from the editor. Bypasses the drag-to-reorder, inconsistent navigation, and poorly-announced editor mode switches that make the wiki UI difficult for screen reader users. ([Community-Access/accessibility-agents](https://github.com/Community-Access/accessibility-agents/blob/HEAD/claude-code-plugin/agents/wiki-manager.md) · 2 copies)
- **wiki-moc-splitter**: Splits an oversized MOC into themed sub-MOCs and turns the parent into an index-of-sub-MOCs hub. Dispatched by autoimprove when the moc-size lint check reports a MOC over the hard cap, or directly when the user asks to split a MOC. ([noopz/commonplace](https://github.com/noopz/commonplace/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-moc-splitter.md) · ★3)
- **wiki-moc-updater**: Keeps Maps of Content (MOCs) in sync with the source notes that reference them — adds missing source entries, updates counts, and fixes stale links. Dispatched after wiki-ingest creates a new source note, and by autoimprove for stale-MOC rounds. ([noopz/commonplace](https://github.com/noopz/commonplace/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-moc-updater.md) · ★3)
- **wiki-module-writer**: llm-wiki agent that writes one module, concept, reference, or data-model page. Authors its own sequence/flow diagrams when useful. ([MisterVitoPro/llm-wiki](https://github.com/MisterVitoPro/llm-wiki/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-module-writer.md))
- **wiki-note**: Adds, edits, or annotates pages in the repo-local markdown wiki. Use for tasks like "add a note to the wiki about X", "log this to the wiki", "record this decision". - **wiki-onboarder**: Generates a tailored reading path through the wiki for a new dev/agent. Invoke via /wiki-onboard --profile &lt;type&gt;. ([agustincbajo/Coral](https://github.com/agustincbajo/Coral/blob/HEAD/template/agents/wiki-onboarder.md))
- **wiki-overview-writer**: llm-wiki agent that writes the top-level spine pages (home, overview, getting-started, architecture, data-flow, tech-stack, glossary). Generic template parameterized per invocation with one page's title, type, section outline, scoped source contents, and any Mermaid diagram blocks to embed. Writes exactly one Markdown page to its owned path. ([MisterVitoPro/llm-wiki](https://github.com/MisterVitoPro/llm-wiki/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-overview-writer.md))
- **wiki-page**: Internal per-topic page writer for nexis:wiki. - **wiki-page-reviewer**: Reviews wiki pages against the wiki-page-author skill. Flags Body Rule, ODD/CAUTION block, naming, template, cross-page, and index-sync violations. ([sheriff-stuff/Scribe-Skills](https://github.com/sheriff-stuff/Scribe-Skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/wiki-page-author/agents/wiki-page-reviewer.md))
- **wiki-page-writer**: Generates one wiki page (category or detail) for a codebase wiki, given a subsystem name, source paths, indexed commit SHA, and repo URL. Reads source files, produces a single markdown page with Mermaid diagrams and commit-pinned source citations, writes it to a specified output path, and returns that path. Should be dispatched many at once — each invocation is independent. ([yorch/claude-skills](https://github.com/yorch/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-page-writer.md))
- **wiki-planner**: llm-wiki agent that plans the wiki. Reads the pre-computed substrate (file index, module boundaries, tech stack, and a code-atlas graph when present) and returns a file-disjoint page plan: a spine of guaranteed pages plus discovered module and concept pages, grouped into waves of &lt;=6 writers ordered so overview pages land first for later cross-linking. ([MisterVitoPro/llm-wiki](https://github.com/MisterVitoPro/llm-wiki/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-planner.md))
- **wiki-planner**: Fast research planner. Turns research questions into a ranked list of source URLs to fetch. Uses WebSearch (or Firecrawl search when enabled). Does not fetch page bodies — returns URLs only. ([mugenjoka1234/llm-wiki](https://github.com/mugenjoka1234/llm-wiki/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/wiki-planner.md))
- **wiki-pruner**: Removes low-value concept stubs from the vault and cleans up their references. Executes the deterministic decisions made by `commonplace prune` — never makes value judgments. Refuses to prune retired notes (routes to wiki-supersede instead). Dispatched by autoimprove during pruning rounds. ([noopz/commonplace](https://github.com/noopz/commonplace/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-pruner.md) · ★3)
- **wiki-querier**: Answer a free-form question against the knowledge wiki and file a cited answer to the explorations folder. May call the configured index CLI for passage-level grounding. - **wiki-query**: Use this agent when searching the vault and synthesizing answers during automated research cycles. - **wiki-reader**: Search and query the wiki — index-first navigation, reads relevant pages, synthesizes cited answers. Automatically researches if wiki doesn't have the answer. ([Oshayr/LLM-Wiki](https://github.com/Oshayr/LLM-Wiki/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-reader.md) · ★50)
- **wiki-reflector**: Use this agent when a problem could benefit from historical judgment, proportionality checks, or cautionary precedent found in Wikipedia. - **wiki-reingest**: Use this agent when detecting and acting on source drift during automated maintenance cycles. Typical triggers include dev-loop IDLE DISCOVERY drift checks, periodic source freshness verification, or post-ingest drift monitoring. See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios. ([karlorz/llm-wiki](https://github.com/karlorz/llm-wiki/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-reingest.md) · ★5 · 2 copies)
- **wiki-researcher**: Expert code analyst conducting systematic deep research with zero tolerance for shallow analysis — traces actual code paths and grounds every claim in evidence ([microsoft/skills](https://github.com/microsoft/skills/blob/HEAD/.github/plugins/deep-wiki/agents/wiki-researcher.md) · ★3k · 5 copies)
- **wiki-researcher**: Use this agent when the user wants to investigate a topic from scratch — Researcher does outbound web search, captures sources, ingests into typed wiki pages with confidence scoring, cross-links, and detects contradictions. - **wiki-researcher**: Use PROACTIVELY when ingesting a source into a council-pattern wiki. Extracts atomic claims, entities, and concepts from a single raw document. Read-only — never writes wiki pages itself. Always paired with wiki-skeptic in parallel. ([Ali-Traboulsi/council-vault](https://github.com/Ali-Traboulsi/council-vault/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/wiki-researcher.md))
- **wiki-researcher**: Searches the Conductor documentation wiki for a topic and synthesizes a cited answer. Read-only retrieval subagent — orients via overview/index, routes to scoped docs, greps + graph-expands [[wikilinks]], ranks by signal density, returns a synthesized answer with [[wikilink]] citations and a source list. ([AtaraxiaEpiphany/conductor](https://github.com/AtaraxiaEpiphany/conductor/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-researcher.md))
- **wiki-researcher**: Reads already-fetched source snapshots and synthesizes an answer with URL citations. No web access — fetching happens in a prior stage. - **wiki-researcher**: Read-only wiki research subagent for evidence-first repository investigation and synthesis. ([RocketSurgeonsGuild/Conventions](https://github.com/RocketSurgeonsGuild/Conventions/blob/HEAD/.apm/agents/wiki-researcher.md) · 3 copies)
- **wiki-reviewer**: Phase-7 zero-network structural quality reviewer for the inverted pipeline. - **wiki-reviewer**: Optional llm-wiki agent that adversarially verifies one already-written page against the source files it claims to document. Re-reads the page and its source_files and flags claims the source does not support (hallucinations, overstatements, stale assertions), plus material facts the page omits. Returns structured findings; writes no files. ([MisterVitoPro/llm-wiki](https://github.com/MisterVitoPro/llm-wiki/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-reviewer.md))
- **wiki-scan**: Internal index-shard worker for nexis:wiki. Scans a slice of .nexis/index.md and returns compact clustering stats (survey) or note→topic labels (assign). Not for direct use — spawned by /nexis:wiki on large stores. ([noetaris-lab/nexis](https://github.com/noetaris-lab/nexis/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-scan.md))
- **wiki-scout**: Read-only repository discovery agent for wijzer. - **wiki-scribe**: Use this agent when the user gives you something to file (URL, PDF, GitHub link, clipboard text, conversation log) and wants it captured into raw/ + wiki/sources/. - **wiki-scribe**: Use during the /wiki-self-update loop to synthesize ONE changed source into its affected docs/wiki/ page(s). The agent reads the source at HEAD plus the named exemplar pages, then makes the minimal SCHEMA-conformant page edit — one concept, kebab slug, bidirectional [[backlinks]], path:line/symbol cites, [synthesis] tags, the audience stamp. It edits pages only; it never grades its own work (a separate wiki-grounding-auditor does that), advances .last_ingest_sha, touches index.md/log.md, or commits — those are the orchestrator's steps. - **wiki-scribe**: 긴 소스(논문, 트랜스크립트, PDF, 문서)를 전부 읽고 그것으로부터 위키 페이지를 작성하거나 확장한다. 흡수(ingest) 과정에서 소스를 전부 읽으면 메인 컨텍스트가 좁아지는 경우에 사용. raw/ 경로, 인접한 기존 페이지, 이미 결정된 페이지 집합을 넘길 것. - **wiki-skeptic**: Use PROACTIVELY in parallel with wiki-researcher when ingesting a source into a council-pattern wiki. ([Ali-Traboulsi/council-vault](https://github.com/Ali-Traboulsi/council-vault/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/wiki-skeptic.md))
- **wiki-spider**: BFS traversal of the wiki DAG from an entry node to produce a structured brief about a feature or entity. - **wiki-sub-query**: ../../../agents/llm-wiki-mgmt/wiki-sub-query.md ([ISaidHey/isaidhey-agent-skills](https://github.com/ISaidHey/isaidhey-agent-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/llm-wiki-mgmt/agents/wiki-sub-query.md))
- **wiki-sync**: Use this agent when syncing the vault git repository during automated maintenance cycles. ([karlorz/llm-wiki](https://github.com/karlorz/llm-wiki/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-sync.md) · ★5 · 2 copies)
- **wiki-sync**: Read-only diff-mapper that identifies which pages in MUDdown.wiki/ need updates after a code change. Use after shipping a feature, command, world content, or protocol change to determine the doc impact. Returns a list of wiki pages that should be touched, with a one-line reason for each. - **wiki-sync**: ../../.github/agents/wiki-sync.md ([MUDdown/MUDdown](https://github.com/MUDdown/MUDdown/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/wiki-sync.md) · ★2)

- **wiki-synthesizer**: Specialized agent for generating cross-cutting insights, identifying patterns, and discovering knowledge gaps across the knowledge base. ([bradduy/wiki-knowledge-compiler](https://github.com/bradduy/wiki-knowledge-compiler/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-synthesizer.md) · ★11)
- **wiki-synthesizer**: You are a cross-page analyst for a personal knowledge base. Your job is to find connections, patterns, and insights that no single wiki page surfaces on its own. - **wiki-synthesizer**: Use after wiki-researcher and wiki-skeptic have returned on the same source. - **wiki-synthesizer**: Phase 2 of the doc-sync pipeline. Regenerates conductor/overview.md from the loaded corpus, co-edits conductor/purpose.md (LLM-maintained sections only), appends the change log, runs the inline drift gate with auto-repair of auto-owned files, and commits. Runs after corpus-writer (Phase 1). Automatic — no user confirmation. ([AtaraxiaEpiphany/conductor](https://github.com/AtaraxiaEpiphany/conductor/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-synthesizer.md))
- **wiki-synthesizer**: Cross-page synthesizer. Reads multiple wiki pages (filtered by query or type) and produces a synthesis digest with wikilink citations. NEVER modifies an existing page — synthesis always lands as a new digest. (Destructive overview rewriting is deferred to v1.1.) ([mugenjoka1234/llm-wiki](https://github.com/mugenjoka1234/llm-wiki/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/wiki-synthesizer.md))

- **wire-all-three-agent-interfaces**: An AL agent type is registered by extending `Agent Metadata Provider`. The platform locates factory, metadata, and task-execution behaviour only through the `Implementation` property on that enum value. Omitting `IAgentFactory`, `IAgentMetadata`, or `IAgentTaskExecution` leaves create, UI identity, or task runs unbound. Models often ship a single codeunit and skip the enum wiring. ([microsoft/BCQuality](https://github.com/microsoft/BCQuality/blob/HEAD/community/knowledge/agents/wire-all-three-agent-interfaces.md) · ★213)
- **wire-api-extractor**: Extracts the external/network API surface of a module: HTTP routes, gRPC services, GraphQL schemas, AsyncAPI / message-broker topics. Prefers spec artifacts (OpenAPI, .proto, .graphql) and framework introspection over greps; falls back to grepping route-registration patterns. Distinct from api-surface-extractor, which covers in-process language symbols. ([mtrense/skills](https://github.com/mtrense/skills/blob/HEAD/codebase-survey/agents/wire-api-extractor.md) · ★3)
